# Outcome coverage, Mathematics Grades 1 to 9 (2026-11-13)

Generated by `npm run coverage:outcomes` (`scripts/coverage-outcomes.mjs`). Free and local: no model calls. Counts and outcome codes only.

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/mathematics/g<N>.json`. A **family** is a hand-written question family (1664 in the bank, 52 switched on for children, the rest waiting for a teacher). A **generator skill** is a skill in the practice engine tagged to the outcome. **Misconception feedback** means a draw carries a message for a named wrong answer. **Worked example** means the skill draws one. Coverage is of what exists in the bank, signed off or not.

Grades 1 to 3 are tagged to the sub-strand, not to an outcome, so they show no outcome-level cover here; the last column counts the outcomes whose sub-strand has any family or skill.

## Outcomes by grade

Columns: F family, S generator skill, M misconception feedback, W worked example, L library entry (Grades 4 and 5). `.` means none. Outcomes with none of F, S, M, W are listed first.

# Outcome coverage, Science and Technology Grades 4 to 6

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/science/g<N>.json` (KICD, word for word). **Grades 4, 5, 6** have skills, misconceptions and question families (written offline, all draft and switched off until a CBC science and technology teacher signs them off). **Attitude** outcomes cannot be tested by a typed or tapped answer; **hands-on** outcomes (demonstrate, apply) need a practical, and the questions test the reasoning only. Columns: F family, S skill in the curriculum map, M misconception feedback on a drawn wrong answer, W worked example, L the skill has a misconception library entry.

| 5 | 32 | 25 | 25 | 25 | 25 | 25 | 8 | 4 | 20 | 20 | 7 |
| 6 | 34 | 26 | 26 | 26 | 26 | 26 | 8 | 4 | 22 | 22 | 8 |

# Outcome coverage, Agriculture and Nutrition Grades 4 to 9

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/agriculture/g<N>.json` (KICD, word for word). **Grades 4, 5, 6, 7** have skills, misconceptions and question families (written offline, all draft and switched off until a CBC agriculture and nutrition teacher signs them off). **Attitude** outcomes cannot be tested by a typed or tapped answer; **hands-on** outcomes (demonstrate, apply) need a practical, and the questions test the reasoning only. Columns: F family, S skill in the curriculum map, M misconception feedback on a drawn wrong answer, W worked example, L the skill has a misconception library entry.

| Grade | Outcomes | With a family | With a skill | With misconception feedback | With a worked example | With a library entry | Attitude | Hands-on | Testable (neither) | Testable with a family | With none of the four |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 6 | 30 | 11 | 12 | 11 | 11 | 12 | 3 | 1 | 26 | 11 | 18 |
| 7 | 42 | 16 | 16 | 16 | 16 | 16 | 4 | 7 | 31 | 15 | 26 |

| 9 | 33 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 4 | 5 | 24 | 0 | 33 |

# Outcome coverage, Christian Religious Education Grades 1 to 8

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/cre/g<N>.json` (KICD, word for word). **Grades 4, 5, 6, 7** have skills, misconceptions and question families (written offline, all draft and switched off until a CBC cre teacher signs them off). **Attitude** outcomes cannot be tested by a typed or tapped answer; **hands-on** outcomes (demonstrate, apply) need a practical, and the questions test the reasoning only. Columns: F family, S skill in the curriculum map, M misconception feedback on a drawn wrong answer, W worked example, L the skill has a misconception library entry.

# Outcome coverage, Creative Arts Grades 4 to 9

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/creative_arts/g<N>.json` (KICD, word for word). **Grades 4, 5, 6, 7** have skills, misconceptions and question families (written offline, all draft and switched off until a CBC creative arts teacher signs them off). **Attitude** outcomes cannot be tested by a typed or tapped answer; **hands-on** outcomes (demonstrate, apply) need a practical, and the questions test the reasoning only. Columns: F family, S skill in the curriculum map, M misconception feedback on a drawn wrong answer, W worked example, L the skill has a misconception library entry.

| Grade | Outcomes | With a family | With a skill | With misconception feedback | With a worked example | With a library entry | Attitude | Hands-on | Testable (neither) | Testable with a family | With none of the four |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 6 | 59 | 21 | 30 | 21 | 21 | 30 | 13 | 2 | 44 | 21 | 29 |
| 7 | 73 | 28 | 29 | 28 | 28 | 29 | 13 | 5 | 55 | 27 | 44 |

| 9 | 67 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 12 | 4 | 51 | 0 | 67 |

# Outcome coverage, Integrated Science Grades 7 to 9

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/integrated_science/g<N>.json` (KICD, word for word). **Grades 7, 8, 9** have skills, misconceptions and question families (written offline, all draft and switched off until a CBC integrated science teacher signs them off). **Attitude** outcomes cannot be tested by a typed or tapped answer; **hands-on** outcomes (demonstrate, apply) need a practical, and the questions test the reasoning only. Columns: F family, S skill in the curriculum map, M misconception feedback on a drawn wrong answer, W worked example, L the skill has a misconception library entry.

| Grade | Outcomes | With a family | With a skill | With misconception feedback | With a worked example | With a library entry | Attitude | Hands-on | Testable (neither) | Testable with a family | With none of the four |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 7 | 38 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 29 | 9 | 1 | 28 | 28 | 9 |
| 8 | 37 | 28 | 28 | 28 | 28 | 28 | 8 | 3 | 26 | 25 | 9 |
| 9 | 46 | 34 | 35 | 34 | 34 | 35 | 8 | 4 | 34 | 32 | 11 |

# Outcome coverage, Pre-Technical Studies Grades 7 to 9

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/pre_technical/g<N>.json` (KICD, word for word). **Grades 7, 8** have skills, misconceptions and question families (written offline, all draft and switched off until a CBC pre-technical studies teacher signs them off). **Attitude** outcomes cannot be tested by a typed or tapped answer; **hands-on** outcomes (demonstrate, apply) need a practical, and the questions test the reasoning only. Columns: F family, S skill in the curriculum map, M misconception feedback on a drawn wrong answer, W worked example, L the skill has a misconception library entry.

| Grade | Outcomes | With a family | With a skill | With misconception feedback | With a worked example | With a library entry | Attitude | Hands-on | Testable (neither) | Testable with a family | With none of the four |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 7 | 58 | 36 | 36 | 36 | 36 | 36 | 11 | 1 | 46 | 35 | 22 |
| 8 | 60 | 36 | 36 | 36 | 36 | 36 | 10 | 3 | 47 | 36 | 24 |
| 9 | 55 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 8 | 2 | 45 | 0 | 55 |

# Outcome coverage, Social Studies Grades 4 to 9

Official outcomes come from `curriculum/social_studies/g<N>.json` (KICD, word for word). **Grades 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9** have skills, misconceptions and question families (written offline, all draft and switched off until a CBC social studies teacher signs them off). **Attitude** outcomes cannot be tested by a typed or tapped answer; **hands-on** outcomes (demonstrate, apply) need a practical, and the questions test the reasoning only. Columns: F family, S skill in the curriculum map, M misconception feedback on a drawn wrong answer, W worked example, L the skill has a misconception library entry.

| Grade | Outcomes | With a family | With a skill | With misconception feedback | With a worked example | With a library entry | Attitude | Hands-on | Testable (neither) | Testable with a family | With none of the four |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
